1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any homologies module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 829. The projective resolution has length at most 659 with Betti numbers bounded by 4.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the homologies space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 829 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 659. QED.
